PW-153 (post-wizard-specs.spec.ts :1876-:1947) selects m2, m1, m2, m1 with UI waits between, then `expect.poll(attributesOf(listingId)[model]).toBe(m1)` and at once openAtReview() → a hard `gotoReady(page, /post/<id>)` (:1730-:1741). On a slow lane the selects spread over more than 2 s, so the timer fires mid-sequence: S_a{m1} after the second select, S_b{m2} after the third, and the fourth select's save is still in its 2-second debounce when the poll reads the DB — which already holds m1 FROM S_a — and passes; the navigation tears the page down with S_b{m2} in flight (or just landed) and the final {m1} never sent → the review opens on m2. The same evidence turn 27/28 read ("the poll saw m1 stored, the review opened on m2") fits exactly; the Flake check on an idle runner cannot reproduce it (all four selects fall inside one debounce window → one save). The product never stored an earlier answer over a later one; the test's wait condition is wrong — DB == m1 is satisfied by an intermediate state, and a hard navigation during a pending debounce loses the last change (the wizard's own navigation flushes first). **INC-809 reclassified back to TEST** (turn 27's reclassification to product rested on the same evidence read without the debounce): the fix is the test's wait — before polling or navigating, wait for `post-save-state` data-state="saved" (wizard.tsx :1095-:1100; the pattern of post-wizard-category.spec.ts :174, :215, :839) — and the class (G29): every spec that navigates or reads DB truth right after a wizard change without awaiting "saved" (census of openAtReview/gotoReady/page.goto/attributesOf after a change in e2e/post-wizard-*.spec.ts).
